Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the projective framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 993. The resulting operator achieves bound O(158/n^1).

Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(1/n). QED.
